# Julia would spend more time optimizing the long straight-line functions
# below than running them takes: they are interpreted, and Alea's own

Base.Experimental.@compiler_options compile=min optimize=0 infer=false

const EV = Ref{AnyBool}(true)
observe!(path, c) = (EV[] = EV[] & (!path | c); true)

function shr(x::DistUInt{W}, n::Integer) where W
    k = min(n, W)
    DistUInt{W}(vcat(fill(false, k), x.bits[1:W - k]))
end

components(x::Tuple) = reduce(vcat, map(components, x); init = Any[])
components(x) = Any[x]
width(::DistUInt{W}) where W = W

function warmup()
    a = flip(0.7)
    x = discrete(DistUInt{2}, [0.1, 0.2, 0.3, 0.4])
    y = uniform(DistUInt{3}, 1, 6)
    z = ifelse(a, x + DistUInt{2}(1), x - DistUInt{2}(1))
    t = ifelse(flip(0.4), (a, z), (!a, x * x))
    e = prob_equals(z, DistUInt{2}(2)) | (x < z) | xor(a, flip(0.2))
    pr(flip(0.7))
    pr(a, z, y, t...; evidence = e)
    nothing
end

     x1648_x_15_13 = x1544_x_14_13 === true ? x1646_x_15_13 : x1544_x_14_13 === false ? x1647_x_15_13 : ifelse(x1544_x_14_13, x1646_x_15_13, x1647_x_15_13)
    end
    if x1645_x_15_12 !== true
     if x1544_x_14_13 !== false
      x1649_x_15_13 = flip(0.5000005)
     end
     if x1544_x_14_13 !== true
      x1650_x_15_13 = flip(0.5000006)
     end
     x1651_x_15_13 = x1544_x_14_13 === true ? x1649_x_15_13 : x1544_x_14_13 === false ? x1650_x_15_13 : ifelse(x1544_x_14_13, x1649_x_15_13, x1650_x_15_13)
    end
    x1652_x_15_13 = x1645_x_15_12 === true ? x1648_x_15_13 : x1645_x_15_12 === false ? x1651_x_15_13 : ifelse(x1645_x_15_12, x1648_x_15_13, x1651_x_15_13)
    if x1652_x_15_13 !== false
     if x1551_x_14_14 !== false
      x1653_x_15_14 = flip(0.5000003)
     end
     if x1551_x_14_14 !== true
      x1654_x_15_14 = flip(0.5000004)
     end
     x1655_x_15_14 = x1551_x_14_14 === true ? x1653_x_15_14 : x1551_x_14_14 === false ? x1654_x_15_14 : ifelse(x1551_x_14_14, x1653_x_15_14, x1654_x_15_14)
    end
    if x1652_x_15_13 !== true
     if x1551_x_14_14 !== false
      x1656_x_15_14 = flip(0.5000005)
     end
     if x1551_x_14_14 !== true
      x1657_x_15_14 = flip(0.5000006)
     end
     x1658_x_15_14 = x1551_x_14_14 === true ? x1656_x_15_14 : x1551_x_14_14 === false ? x1657_x_15_14 : ifelse(x1551_x_14_14, x1656_x_15_14, x1657_x_15_14)
    end
    x1659_x_15_14 = x1652_x_15_13 === true ? x1655_x_15_14 : x1652_x_15_13 === false ? x1658_x_15_14 : ifelse(x1652_x_15_13, x1655_x_15_14, x1658_x_15_14)
    if x1659_x_15_14 !== false
     if x1558_x_14_15 !== false
      x1660_x_15_15 = flip(0.5000003)
     end
     if x1558_x_14_15 !== true
      x1661_x_15_15 = flip(0.5000004)
     end
     x1662_x_15_15 = x1558_x_14_15 === true ? x1660_x_15_15 : x1558_x_14_15 === false ? x1661_x_15_15 : ifelse(x1558_x_14_15, x1660_x_15_15, x1661_x_15_15)
    end
    if x1659_x_15_14 !== true
     if x1558_x_14_15 !== false
      x1663_x_15_15 = flip(0.5000005)
     end
     if x1558_x_14_15 !== true
      x1664_x_15_15 = flip(0.5000006)
     end
     x1665_x_15_15 = x1558_x_14_15 === true ? x1663_x_15_15 : x1558_x_14_15 === false ? x1664_x_15_15 : ifelse(x1558_x_14_15, x1663_x_15_15, x1664_x_15_15)
    end
    x1666_x_15_15 = x1659_x_15_14 === true ? x1662_x_15_15 : x1659_x_15_14 === false ? x1665_x_15_15 : ifelse(x1659_x_15_14, x1662_x_15_15, x1665_x_15_15)
    return x1666_x_15_15
end

function dice_main()
    (x293_x_3_4, x300_x_3_5, x307_x_3_6, x314_x_3_7, x321_x_3_8, x328_x_3_9, x335_x_3_10, x342_x_3_11, x349_x_3_12, x356_x_3_13, x363_x_3_14, x370_x_3_15, x373_x_4_0, x380_x_4_1, x387_x_4_2, x394_x_4_3,) = chunk_1()
    (x687_x_6_14, x694_x_6_15, x697_x_7_0, x704_x_7_1, x711_x_7_2, x718_x_7_3, x725_x_7_4, x732_x_7_5, x739_x_7_6, x746_x_7_7, x753_x_7_8, x760_x_7_9, x767_x_7_10, x774_x_7_11, x781_x_7_12, x788_x_7_13,) = chunk_2(x293_x_3_4, x300_x_3_5, x307_x_3_6, x314_x_3_7, x321_x_3_8, x328_x_3_9, x335_x_3_10, x342_x_3_11, x349_x_3_12, x356_x_3_13, x363_x_3_14, x370_x_3_15, x373_x_4_0, x380_x_4_1, x387_x_4_2, x394_x_4_3)
    (x1084_x_10_9, x1091_x_10_10, x1098_x_10_11, x1105_x_10_12, x1112_x_10_13, x1119_x_10_14, x1126_x_10_15, x1129_x_11_0, x1136_x_11_1, x1143_x_11_2, x1150_x_11_3, x1157_x_11_4, x1164_x_11_5, x1171_x_11_6, x1178_x_11_7, x1185_x_11_8,) = chunk_3(x687_x_6_14, x694_x_6_15, x697_x_7_0, x704_x_7_1, x711_x_7_2, x718_x_7_3, x725_x_7_4, x732_x_7_5, x739_x_7_6, x746_x_7_7, x753_x_7_8, x760_x_7_9, x767_x_7_10, x774_x_7_11, x781_x_7_12, x788_x_7_13)
    (x1481_x_14_4, x1488_x_14_5, x1495_x_14_6, x1502_x_14_7, x1509_x_14_8, x1516_x_14_9, x1523_x_14_10, x1530_x_14_11, x1537_x_14_12, x1544_x_14_13, x1551_x_14_14, x1558_x_14_15, x1582_x_15_3,) = chunk_4(x1084_x_10_9, x1091_x_10_10, x1098_x_10_11, x1105_x_10_12, x1112_x_10_13, x1119_x_10_14, x1126_x_10_15, x1129_x_11_0, x1136_x_11_1, x1143_x_11_2, x1150_x_11_3, x1157_x_11_4, x1164_x_11_5, x1171_x_11_6, x1178_x_11_7, x1185_x_11_8)
    x1666_x_15_15 = chunk_5(x1481_x_14_4, x1488_x_14_5, x1495_x_14_6, x1502_x_14_7, x1509_x_14_8, x1516_x_14_9, x1523_x_14_10, x1530_x_14_11, x1537_x_14_12, x1544_x_14_13, x1551_x_14_14, x1558_x_14_15, x1582_x_15_3)
    return x1666_x_15_15
end

function report(k, x, d)
    if x isa AnyBool
        println("alea ", k, " true ", d[true])
        println("alea ", k, " false ", d[false])
    else
        for v in 0:(2^width(x) - 1)
            println("alea ", k, " ", v, " ", d[v])
        end
    end
end

function run_program()
    EV[] = true
    stats = @timed begin
        comps = components(dice_main())
        dists = pr(comps...; evidence = EV[])
        (comps, length(comps) == 1 ? [dists] : dists)
    end
    comps, dists = stats.value
    for k in eachindex(comps)
        report(k, comps[k], dists[k])
    end
    println("alea_seconds ", stats.time)
    println("alea_compile_seconds ", get(stats, :compile_time, NaN))
end

warmup()
task = Task(run_program, 1 << 30)
schedule(task)
wait(task)
end
